// Why It Matters
DATA LIABILITY IS REAL.

Improperly disposed electronics are one of the most common sources of data breaches for small businesses. A Certificate of Destruction is your proof you handled it correctly — and we bring it to your door.

  • Mobile service — we come directly to your office or site
  • Physical destruction available for hard drives and SSDs
  • Certificate issued per device or per batch
  • Suitable for HIPAA, legal, and financial compliance documentation
  • AI setup by a certified cybersecurity professional
  • MDM deployment — Intune, Jamf, MobileIron
  • Baltimore-based — you know exactly who you're dealing with
